You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ef7c8ece-3f82-4464-b70c-2257c3cc8ccf","uuid":"e7e948de-74ee-4009-a40c-d6fa23f83f84","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/app/project/docs/architecture/api_gateway.md","content":"```markdown\n# SynestheticCanvas ‑ API Gateway Architecture  \n*(Module: `api_graphql`)*  \n\n> “Treat every endpoint as a brush stroke.”  \n> — Lead Architect, SynestheticCanvas\n\n---\n\n## 1. Purpose\n\nThe API Gateway is the single ingress/egress point for **all** client traffic—GraphQL, REST fall-backs, metrics, health checks, and WebSocket subscriptions.  \nIt **does not** implement business logic; it orchestrates:\n\n* Request validation & normalization  \n* Schema version negotiation  \n* Dynamic routing to the appropriate microservice  \n* Response shaping, pagination, and caching  \n* Observability (structured logging, tracing, metrics)  \n* Rate-limiting and traffic shaping  \n\n---\n\n## 2. High-Level Diagram\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph Gateway\n        A(API Edge) -- gRPC / HTTP --> R[Router]\n        R -- GraphQL --> GQ(GraphQL Engine)\n        R -- REST --> RS(REST Adapter)\n        R -- WS --> WS(WebSocket Hub)\n    end\n\n    GQ -- Async JSON/gRPC --> Microservices\n    RS -- HTTP --> Microservices\n    WS -- Pub/Sub --> Microservices\n\n    Microservices -- gRPC/HTTP --> Datastores[(Data Stores)]\n    subgraph Observability\n        L(Log Aggregator)\n        M(Metrics)\n        T(Tracing)\n    end\n    Gateway -- logs --> L\n    Gateway -- metrics --> M\n    Gateway -- spans --> T\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Technology Stack\n\n| Concern            | Technology / Library                               |\n| ------------------ | -------------------------------------------------- |\n| HTTP / HTTP/2      | [libh2o](https://github.com/h2o/h2o)               |\n| GraphQL Engine     | [cgql](https://github.com/graphql-c/cgql)          |\n| JSON Parsing       | [yyjson](https://github.com/ibireme/yyjson)        |\n| gRPC Client        | [gRPC-C](https://github.com/Juniper/grpc-c)        |\n| WebSocket          | Built-in H2O WS API                                |\n| Caching            | pluggable: Redis, in-process LRU                   |\n| Metrics            | [Prometheus-C](https://github.com/digitalocean/prometheus-client-c)|\n| Tracing            | OpenTelemetry C SDK                                |\n| Logging            | [spdlog-c](https://github.com/irsl/spdlog-c)       |\n\n> All third-party dependencies are vendored and version-pinned via `cmake/FetchContent` to ensure reproducibility.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Request Life-Cycle\n\n1. **Ingress**  \n   – `libh2o` accepts the TCP/TLS connection and performs ALPN (HTTP/2, HTTP/1.1, WebSocket).  \n2. **Pre-flight Filters**  \n   – IP allow/deny, rate limiter, TLS client-certificate check.  \n3. **Protocol Router**  \n   – Based on `:path` and `Content-Type` headers:  \n     * `/graphql` → GraphQL handler  \n     * `/v{N}/**`  → REST adapter (versioned)  \n     * `/ws`       → WebSocket Hub  \n4. **Validation**  \n   – JSON Schema (REST) or GraphQL AST validation, depth-limit, complexity analyzer.  \n5. **Service Discovery**  \n   – Resolve microservice target via Consul or static registry.  \n6. **Proxy / Execution**  \n   – Forward the request (gRPC or HTTP).  \n   – Collect partial responses for **federated** GraphQL queries.  \n7. **Response Filters**  \n   – Pagination, error redaction, ETag/caching headers.  \n   – Observability emit (metrics, traces).  \n8. **Egress**  \n   – Send response, close connection if needed.  \n\n---\n\n## 5. Key Design Decisions\n\n### 5.1 Versioned GraphQL Schemas\n\nSchemas are **append-only**; breaking changes require a new major (`v2`, `v3`, …).  \nGateway negotiates version through:\n\n1. `X-SC-GraphQL-Schema: vN` header (preferred)  \n2. `SC-GQL-Schema` cookie (Web clients)  \n3. Fallback to the greatest supported version.  \n\n### 5.2 REST as a Compatibility Layer\n\nEvery GraphQL field with `@rest` directive automatically spawns a REST endpoint during build:\n\n```graphql\ntype Palette {\n    id: ID!\n    name: String! @rest(path: \"/v1/palettes/{id}\", method: GET)\n    colors: [Color!]!\n}\n```\n\nThe code-gen step creates a façade function in `rest_adapter.c` that marshals to the GraphQL resolver internally (see §6).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Core Source Walk-through\n\nBelow are trimmed but **compilable** excerpts that illustrate best practices.\n\n### 6.1 gateway.c – Entry Point\n\n```c\n/**\n * gateway.c\n * ----------\n * SynestheticCanvas API Gateway entry point.\n * Bootstraps the web server, loads plugin modules, \n * and wires observability hooks.\n */\n#include <h2o.h>\n#include <cgql/cgql.h>\n#include <yyjson.h>\n#include <prom.h>\n#include <signal.h>\n\n#include \"config.h\"\n#include \"router.h\"\n#include \"observability.h\"\n\nstatic h2o_globalconf_t   g_config;\nstatic h2o_context_t      g_ctx;\nstatic h2o_multithread_queue_t *g_queue = NULL;\n\nstatic void on_sigterm(int signo) {\n    h2o_context_request_shutdown(&g_ctx);\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char **argv)\n{\n    // 1. Parse CLI / ENV configuration\n    sc_config_t cfg = {0};\n    if (sc_config_load(&cfg, argc, argv) != 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Failed to load configuration\\n\");\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Initialize logging ASAP\n    sc_log_init(cfg.log_level, cfg.log_file);\n\n    // 3. Observability stack\n    if (sc_observe_init(&cfg.observe) != 0) {\n        SC_LOG_ERROR(\"Observability init failed\");\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    // 4. Setup H2O global configuration\n    h2o_config_init(&g_config);\n    sc_router_register(&g_config, &cfg);\n\n    // 5. Create listener sockets\n    int listener_fd = sc_socket_create_listener(cfg.bind_address, cfg.port);\n    if (listener_fd < 0) {\n        SC_LOG_ERROR(\"Cannot bind %s:%d\", cfg.bind_address, cfg.port);\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    // 6. Initialize thread pool\n    h2o_context_init(&g_ctx, h2o_evloop_create(), &g_config);\n    g_queue = h2o_multithread_create_queue(&g_ctx, \"sc-queue\");\n\n    // 7. Signal handling\n    signal(SIGTERM, on_sigterm);\n    signal(SIGINT,  on_sigterm);\n\n    SC_LOG_INFO(\"SynestheticCanvas Gateway started on %s:%d\", \n                cfg.bind_address, cfg.port);\n\n    // 8. Main event loop\n    while (h2o_evloop_run(g_ctx.loop, INT32_MAX) == 0);\n\n    // 9. Graceful shutdown\n    h2o_context_dispose(&g_ctx);\n    h2o_config_dispose(&g_config);\n    sc_observe_shutdown();\n    sc_config_free(&cfg);\n\n    SC_LOG_INFO(\"Gateway shut down gracefully\");\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```\n\n### 6.2 router.c – URL Multiplexer\n\n```c\n/**\n * router.c\n * --------\n * Declarative URL routing. New routes are appended\n * here by code-gen scripts to avoid scattered logic.\n */\n#include \"router.h\"\n#include \"handlers/graphql_handler.h\"\n#include \"handlers/rest_handler.h\"\n#include \"handlers/ws_handler.h\"\n\nvoid sc_router_register(h2o_globalconf_t *glob, const sc_config_t *cfg)\n{\n    h2o_pathconf_t *graphql  = h2o_config_register_path(glob, \"/graphql\", 0);\n    graphql->handlers = (h2o_handler_t *[]){\n        sc_graphql_handler_create(glob, cfg), NULL\n    };\n\n    h2o_pathconf_t *rest_v1  = h2o_config_register_path(glob, \"/v1\", H2O_URL_EGL_LIKELY);\n    rest_v1->handlers = (h2o_handler_t *[]){\n        sc_rest_handler_create(glob, cfg, 1), NULL\n    };\n\n    h2o_pathconf_t *rest_v2  = h2o_config_register_path(glob, \"/v2\", 0);\n    rest_v2->handlers = (h2o_handler_t *[]){\n        sc_rest_handler_create(glob, cfg, 2), NULL\n    };\n\n    h2o_pathconf_t *ws       = h2o_config_register_path(glob, \"/ws\", 0);\n    ws->handlers = (h2o_handler_t *[]){\n        sc_ws_handler_create(glob, cfg), NULL\n    };\n}\n```\n\n### 6.3 graphql_handler.c – Streaming Execution\n\n```c\n/**\n * GraphQL Handler\n */\n#include <cgql/executor.h>\n#include <cgql/validator.h>\n#include \"handlers/graphql_handler.h\"\n#include \"services/federation.h\"\n\ntypedef struct {\n    h2o_handler_t super;\n    sc_config_t  *cfg;\n} sc_graphql_handler_t;\n\nstatic int on_req(h2o_handler_t *self, h2o_req_t *req)\n{\n    sc_graphql_handler_t *h = (sc_graphql_handler_t *)self;\n\n    // 1. Ensure method POST\n    if (req->method.len != 4 || memcmp(req->method.base, \"POST\", 4) != 0) {\n        return h2o_send_error_405(req, \"Method Not Allowed\", \"\", H2O_SEND_ERROR_KEEP_HEADERS);\n    }\n\n    // 2. Parse JSON body\n    yyjson_doc *doc = NULL;\n    if (sc_json_parse_request(req, &doc) != 0) {\n        return h2o_send_error_400(req, \"Invalid JSON\", \"\", 0);\n    }\n\n    // 3. Extract \"query\" and \"variables\"\n    const char *query     = sc_json_get_string(doc, \"query\");\n    yyjson_val *vars_node = yyjson_get(doc, \"variables\");\n\n    // 4. Parse & validate GraphQL\n    cgql_parser_t *parser = cgql_parser_new(query);\n    cgql_ast_t    *ast    = cgql_parse(parser);\n\n    if (!ast) {\n        sc_json_free(doc);\n        cgql_parser_free(parser);\n        return h2o_send_error_400(req, \"GraphQL parse error\", parser->last_error, 0);\n    }\n\n    char *validation_err = NULL;\n    if (!cgql_validate(ast, &validation_err)) {\n        sc_json_free(doc);\n        cgql_ast_free(ast);\n        cgql_parser_free(parser);\n        int res = h2o_send_error_400(req, \"GraphQL validation error\", \n                                     validation_err, 0);\n        free(validation_err);\n        return res;\n    }\n\n    // 5. Execute against federated services\n    yyjson_mut_doc *result = sc_fed_execute(h->cfg, ast, vars_node);\n\n    // 6. Serialize response\n    sc_http_send_json(req, result);\n\n    // Cleanup\n    sc_json_free(doc);\n    yyjson_mut_doc_free(result);\n    cgql_ast_free(ast);\n    cgql_parser_free(parser);\n    return 0;\n}\n\nh2o_handler_t *sc_graphql_handler_create(h2o_globalconf_t *glob, const sc_config_t *cfg)\n{\n    sc_graphql_handler_t *h = h2o_mem_alloc_shared(NULL, sizeof(*h), NULL);\n    h2o_handler_init((h2o_handler_t *)h, sizeof(*h));\n    h->super.on_req = on_req;\n    h->cfg          = (sc_config_t *)cfg;\n    return (h2o_handler_t *)h;\n}\n```\n\n### 6.4 rest_adapter.c – Auto-Generated Example\n\n```c\n/**\n * Auto-generated REST endpoint for Palette retrieval.\n * Mirrors the GraphQL field: Palette.name\n */\n#include \"handlers/rest_handler.h\"\n#include \"proto/palette.pb-c.h\"\n#include \"services/palette_client.h\"\n\nstatic int handle_get_palette(h2o_handler_t *self, h2o_req_t *req)\n{\n    const h2o_iovec_t *id = h2o_req_extract_path_param(req, \"id\");\n    if (!id || id->len == 0) {\n        return h2o_send_error_400(req, \"Missing palette id\", \"\", 0);\n    }\n\n    PaletteRequest pr = PALETTE_REQUEST__INIT;\n    pr.id = strndup(id->base, id->len);\n\n    PaletteResponse *resp = palette_client_get(&pr);\n    free(pr.id);\n\n    if (!resp) {\n        return h2o_send_error_502(req, \"Upstream failure\", \"\", 0);\n    }\n\n    yyjson_mut_doc *doc = yyjson_mut_doc_new(NULL);\n    yyjson_mut_obj_add_str(doc, doc, \"id\", resp->id);\n    yyjson_mut_obj_add_str(doc, doc, \"name\", resp->name);\n\n    sc_http_send_json(req, doc);\n    palette_response__free_unpacked(resp, NULL);\n    yyjson_mut_doc_free(doc);\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Observability Blueprint\n\n1. **Structured Logging** → JSON lines,  `stderr` fallback.  \n2. **Metrics** → Prometheus exposition `/metrics`.  \n3. **Tracing** → OpenTelemetry exporter (Jaeger by default).  \n4. **Health** → `GET /healthz` (liveness) and `/readyz` (readiness).  \n\nExample Prometheus metric naming:\n\n```text\nsc_gateway_request_total{method=\"POST\",endpoint=\"/graphql\",status=\"2xx\"} 135\nsc_gateway_upstream_latency_seconds_bucket{service=\"texture_svc\",le=\"0.1\"} 512\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Resilience & Scaling\n\n* **Circuit Breakers:** Per-service rolling window on failure rate.  \n* **Rate Limiting:** Token bucket via Redis or in-mem fallback.  \n* **Autoscaling Signals:** P95 latency, CPU, custom “brush_stroke” metric.  \n* **Blue/Green Deploy:** Canary header `X-SC-Canary: true`.  \n\n---\n\n## 9. Future Work\n\n* GraphQL defer/stream support (incremental delivery)  \n* WASM plugin sandbox for user-defined resolvers  \n* QUIC/HTTP-3 transport evaluation  \n\n---\n\n## 10. References\n\n* “Building an API Gateway in C”, *C90Con* (2022).  \n* “GraphQL Federation at Scale”, *FOSDEM* (2023).  \n\n---\n\n> **Document ID:** `SC-ARCH-APIGW`  \n> **Last Reviewed:** 2024-05-09  \n> **Maintainers:** `@infra-paint`, `@canvas-ops`\n\n```","numLines":412,"startLine":1,"totalLines":412}}}

222→    size_t needed = strlen(base) + strlen(path) + 2; /* '/' + '\\0' */\n   223→    char *url = malloc(needed);\n   224→    if (!url) return NULL;\n   225→\n   226→    snprintf(url, needed, 263→        resp.mem = malloc(1);\n   264→        resp.len = 0;\n   265→        if (!resp.mem)\n   266→            return SC_ENOMEM;\n   267→\n   268→        pthread_mutex_lock(&client->curl_mtx);\n are retried */\n   314→        LOG_WRN(\"Request attempt %d failed (curlCode=%d, http=%ld) - retrying...\", attempt, res, status);\n   315→\n   316→        free(resp.mem);\n   317→\n   318→        /* realsize;\n}\n\nstatic int perform_request(sc_service_client_t *client,\n                           const char          *url,\n                           const char          *method,\n                           const char          *json_body,\n                           long                *http_status,\n                           cJSON              **out_body)\n{\n    int      attempt = 0;\n    CURLcode res     = CURLE_OK;\n    long     status  = 0;\n    struct timespec ts_sleep = {0};\n    double   backoff_ms = client->backoff_initial_ms;\n\n    do\n    {\n        response_buf_t resp;\n curl_easy_perform(h);\n        if (res == CURLE_OK)\n            curl_easy_getinfo(h, CURLINFO_RESPONSE_CODE, &status);\n\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&client->curl_mtx);\n        curl_slist_free_all(hdrs);\n\n        if (res == CURLE_OK && status >= 200 && status < 500)\n        {\n            *http_status = status;\n\n            if (out_body)\n            {\n jitter) % 1000) * 1000000L;\n        nanosleep(&ts_sleep, NULL);\n        backoff_ms *= client->backoff_factor;\n    }\n    while (++attempt <= client->max_retries);\n\n    *http_status = status;\n Redirects to syslog and stderr (debug builds). */\n#ifndef SC_IPC_LOG_TAG\n#define SC_IPC_LOG_TAG \"sc_ipc\"\n#endif\n\n#define SC_LOG(level, fmt, ...)                                                       \\\n    do {                                                                              \\\n        syslog(level, \"[\" SC_IPC_LOG_TAG \"] \" fmt, ##__VA_ARGS__);                    \\\n        /* In debug/CI builds we additionally emit to stderr for convenience.   */    \\\n        /* NDEBUG disables the extra logging in production to save cycles.      */    \\\n        /* (The syslog call above remains.)                                     */    \\\n        #ifndef NDEBUG                                                                  \\\n        fprintf(stderr, \"[\" SC_IPC_LOG_TAG \"] \" fmt \"\\n\", ##__VA_ARGS__);             \\\n        #endif                                                                         \\\n    } while (0)\n\nstatic inline int\nset_fd_flags(int fd, int set, int unset)\n{\n    int flags = fcntl(fd, F_GETFL, 0);\n    if (flags == -1)\n        return -1;\n    flags |= set;\n    flags &= ~unset;\n    return fcntl(fd, F_SETFL, flags);\n}\n\nstatic int\nset_nonblocking_cloexec(int fd)\n{\n    if (set_fd_flags(fd, O_NONBLOCK, 0) == -1)\n        return -1;\n\n    /* Ensure FD_CLOEXEC to avoid leaking sockets into execve. */\n    int flags = fcntl(fd, F_GETFD, 0);\n    if (flags == -1)\n        return -1;\n    return fcntl(fd, F_SETFD, flags | FD_CLOEXEC);\n}\n\n/* Write exactly len bytes or fail with ‑1. Returns 0 on success. */\nstatic int\nwrite_full(int fd, const uint8_t *buf, size_t len)\n{\n    while (len > 0) {\n        ssize_t n = write(fd, buf, len);\n        if (n < 0) {\n            if (errno == EINTR)\n                continue;\n            if (errno == EAGAIN || errno == EWOULDBLOCK) {\n                /* Busy-wait could be replaced with poll/epoll for high-load\n                 * services. Returns 0, ‑1 on error, ‑2 on\n * timeout. */\nstatic int\nread_full_deadline(int fd, uint8_t *buf, size_t len, int deadline_ms)\n{\n    struct pollfd pfd = { .fd = fd, .events = POLLIN };\n\n    while (len > 0) {\n        int rc = poll(&pfd, 1, deadline_ms);\n        if (rc == 0)\n            return -2; /* timeout                               */\n        if (rc < 0) {\n            if (errno == EINTR)\n                continue;\n            return -1;\n        }\n        ssize_t n = read(fd, buf, len);\n        if (n == 0)\n            return -1; /* orderly shutdown (unexpected here)    */\n        if (n < 0) {\n            if (errno == EINTR)\n                continue;\n            return -1;\n        }\n        buf += n;\n        len -= (size_t)n;\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Public API\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\nstruct sc_ipc_endpoint {\n *socket_path, int backlog)\n{\n    if (!socket_path) {\n        errno = EINVAL;\n        return NULL;\n    }\n    size_t path_len = strlen(socket_path);\n    if (path_len == 0 || path_len >= sizeof(((struct sockaddr_un *)0)->sun_path)) {\n        errno = ENAMETOOLONG;\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    int fd = socket(AF_UNIX, SOCK_STREAM | SOCK_NONBLOCK | SOCK_CLOEXEC, 0);\n    if (fd == -1) {\n        SC_LOG(LOG_ERR, \"socket(): %m\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Remove leftover socket path from previously crashed instance. */\n    unlink(socket_path);\n\n    struct sockaddr_un addr = { .sun_family = AF_UNIX };\n    strncpy(addr.sun_path, socket_path, sizeof(addr.sun_path) - 1);\n\n    if (bind(fd, (struct sockaddr *)&addr, sizeof(addr)) == -1) {\n        SC_LOG(LOG_ERR, \"bind(%s): %m\", socket_path);\n        close(fd);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Restrict permissions to caller's UID/GID (umask honored). */\n    if (chmod(socket_path, 0660) == -1) {\n        SC_LOG(LOG_WARNING, \"chmod(%s): %m\", socket_path);\n        /* Not fatal; continue. */\n    }\n\n    if (listen(fd, backlog) == -1) {\n        SC_LOG(LOG_ERR, \"listen(): endpoint_new(fd, false, socket_path);\n    if (!ep) {\n        close(fd);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n    return ep;\n}\n\nssize_t\nsc_ipc_send(const sc_ipc_endpoint_t *endpoint, const void *buf, size_t len,\n            int flags /* future use */)\n{\n    (void)flags;\n\n    if (!endpoint || !buf || len > SC_IPC_MAX_PAYLOAD) {\n        errno = EINVAL;\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    uint8_t header[4];\n    uint32_t be_len = htonl((uint32_t)len);\n    memcpy(header, &be_len, sizeof(be_len));\n\n    struct iovec iov[2] = {\n        { .iov_base = header, .iov_len = sizeof(header) },\n        { .iov_base = (void *)buf, .iov_len = len },\n    };\n\n    /* Use writev for fewer syscalls */\n    ssize_t sent = writev(endpoint->fd, iov, 2);\n    if (sent == -1 && (errno == EAGAIN || errno == EWOULDBLOCK)) {\n        /* Fallback to blocking loop */\n        if (write_full(endpoint->fd, header, sizeof(header)) == -1)\n            return -1;\n        if (write_full(endpoint->fd, buf, len) == -1)\n            return -1;\n        sent = sizeof(header) + (ssize_t)len;\n    } else if (sent != (ssize_t)sizeof(header) + (ssize_t)len) {\n        /* Partial write not expected when socket is blocking; treat as error. */\n        errno = EIO;\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    return sent;\n}\n\nssize_t\nsc_ipc_recv(const sc_ipc_endpoint_t *endpoint, void **buf_out, int timeout_ms)\n{\n     sizeof(be_len), timeout_ms);\n    if (rc == -2) { /* timeout */\n        return 0;\n    }\n    if (rc == -1) {\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    uint32_t len = ntohl(be_len);\n    if (len == 0 || free(payload);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    payload[len] = '\\0'; /* NUL-terminate for quick string use (optional) */\n    *buf_out     = payload;\n    return (ssize_t)len;\n}\n\nvoid\nsc_ipc_endpoint_destroy(sc_ipc_endpoint_t *endpoint)\n{\n    if (!endpoint)\n        return;\n\n    if (endpoint->fd != -1)\n        close(endpoint->fd);\n\n    /* Remove socket file only for server listening endpoints. */\n    if (endpoint->is_server && *endpoint->path)\n        unlink(endpoint->path);\n\n    free(endpoint);\n}\n\n/* Convenience wrapper to send a <limits.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <uuid/uuid.h>\n\n#include <jansson.h>\n\n#include \"logger.h\"              /* Project-local, syslog-backed logging wrapper */\n#include \"metrics.h\"             /* Histogram / Counter helpers */\n#include \"texture_handler.h\"     /* Corresponding public header */\n#include \"texture_repository.h\"  /* Repository Pattern abstraction */\n#include \"validation.h\"          /* Utility validators for inbound DTOs */\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Internal Constants                                                       */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n#define TEXTURE_ID_STRLEN 37           /* 36 bytes UUID + NUL */\n#define MAX_TEXTURE_NAME_LEN 128\n#define MAX_COLOR_PROFILE_LEN 32\n#define JSON_SERIALIZATION_FLAGS (JSON_COMPACT | JSON_SORT_KEYS)\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Private Helper Structs / Types                                           */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/* Guard state for thread-safe handler */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    TextureRepository *repo;       /* Injected storage adapter (non-null) */\n    MetricsCollector  *metrics;    /* Global metrics sink (may be null)   */\n    pthread_rwlock_t   lock;       /* Protects repository multi-step ops  */\n    bool               ready;      /* Handler has been initialized        */\n} TextureHandlerState;\n\n/* Encoded error detail used for internal propagation */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    TextureErrorCode code;\n    char             msg[256];\n} TextureErrorDetail;\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Local Forward Declarations                                               */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic void        _set_error(TextureError *out, TextureErrorCode code, const char *fmt, ...)\n    __attribute__((format(printf, 3, 4)));\nstatic bool        _validate_texture_input(const TextureInput *in, TextureError *err);\nstatic char       *_generate_uuid(void);\nstatic json_t     *_texture_to_json(const Texture *tx, TextureError *err);\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Public API Implementation                                                */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nTextureHandler *texture_handler_new(TextureRepository *repo, MetricsCollector *metrics, TextureError *err)\n{\n    if (repo == NULL)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_INVALID_ARGUMENT, \"TextureRepository instance cannot be NULL\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    TextureHandlerState *state = calloc(1, sizeof(TextureHandlerState));\n    if (!state)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_OOM, \"Failed to allocate handler state: %s\", strerror(errno));\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    state->repo    = repo;\n    state->metrics = metrics;\n    state->ready   = false;\n\n    if (pthread_rwlock_init(&state->lock, NULL) != 0)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_INTERNAL, \"Failed to initialize rwlock: %s\", strerror(errno));\n        free(state);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    state->ready = true;\n    log_info(\"[texture-handler] Initialized successfully\");\n\n    return (TextureHandler *)state;\n}\n\nvoid texture_handler_dispose(TextureHandler *handler)\n{\n    if (!handler)\n        return;\n\n    TextureHandlerState *state = (TextureHandlerState *)handler;\n    pthread_rwlock_destroy(&state->lock);\n    /* Repository and metrics lifetime are managed by the caller */\n    free(state);\n}\n\n/**\n * Create a new texture based on the DTO provided by higher layers. This\n * function validates all fields, generates an ID, persists the record, and\n * returns a deep-copied Texture object that the caller must free.\n */\nTexture *texture_handler_create(TextureHandler *handler,\n                                const TextureInput *input,\n                                TextureError *err)\n{\n    TextureHandlerState *state = (TextureHandlerState *)handler;\n    if (!state || !state->ready)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_NOT_INITIALIZED, \"TextureHandler is not initialized\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Validate input first */\n    if (!_validate_texture_input(input, err))\n        return NULL;\n\n    /* Build Texture domain object */\n    Texture tx      = {0};\n    tx.id           = _generate_uuid();\n    tx.name         = strndup(input->name, MAX_TEXTURE_NAME_LEN);\n    tx.color_profile= strndup(input->color_profile, MAX_COLOR_PROFILE_LEN);\n    tx.width        = input->width;\n    tx.height       = input->height;\n    tx.created_at   = (uint64_t)time(NULL);\n\n    if (!tx.id || !tx.name || !tx.color_profile)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_OOM, \"Failed to allocate texture attributes\");\n        goto cleanup_fail;\n    }\n\n    /* Serialize metadata payload */\n    tx.metadata = _texture_to_json(&tx, err);\n    if (!tx.metadata)\n        goto cleanup_fail;\n\n    /* Acquire write-lock for repository update */\n    if (pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&state->lock) != 0)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_INTERNAL, \"Failed to acquire write lock\");\n        goto cleanup_fail;\n    }\n\n    bool persist_ok = repository_save_texture(state->repo, &tx, err);\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&state->lock);\n\n    if (!persist_ok)\n        goto cleanup_fail;\n\n    /* Metrics */\n    if (state->metrics)\n        metrics_counter_inc(state->metrics, \"texture_created_total\", 1);\n\n    /* Deep copy result for caller (repository may own original memory) */\n    Texture *result = texture_clone(&tx, err);\n    texture_free(&tx); /* Free local copy */\n\n    log_info(\"[texture-handler] Created texture '%s' (%ux%u)\", result->id, result->width, result->height);\n    return result;\n\ncleanup_fail:\n    texture_free(&tx);\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/**\n * Retrieve a single texture by its UUID. Caller is responsible for freeing\n * the returned object via texture_free().\n */\nTexture *texture_handler_get(TextureHandler *handler, const char *texture_id, TextureError *err)\n{\n    TextureHandlerState *state = (TextureHandlerState *)handler;\n    if (!state || !state->ready)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_NOT_INITIALIZED, \"TextureHandler is not initialized\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    if (!validation_is_uuid(texture_id))\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_INVALID_ARGUMENT, \"Invalid UUID provided: '%s'\", texture_id);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    if (pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&state->lock) != 0)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_INTERNAL, \"Failed to acquire read lock\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    Texture *tx = repository_get_texture(state->repo, texture_id, err);\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&state->lock);\n\n    if (!tx)\n        return NULL; /* Error already populated */\n\n    if (state->metrics)\n        metrics_counter_inc(state->metrics, \"texture_fetched_total\", 1);\n\n    return tx; /* Ownership transferred to caller */\n}\n\n/**\n * Paginated list of textures.  Returns the number of items copied into\n * out_list, or SIZE_MAX on error.\n *\n * The out_list vector is allocated by the caller; this function fills it up to\n * page_req->page_size. Each populated entry must be released with\n * texture_free() by the caller.\n */\nsize_t texture_handler_list(TextureHandler           *handler,\n                            const PaginationRequest  *page_req,\n                            Texture                 **out_list,\n                            TextureError             *err)\n{\n    memset(out_list, 0, sizeof(Texture *) * page_req->page_size);\n\n    TextureHandlerState *state = (TextureHandlerState *)handler;\n    if (!state || !state->ready)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_NOT_INITIALIZED, \"TextureHandler is not initialized\");\n        return SIZE_MAX;\n    }\n\n    if (!page_req || !validation_is_pagination_request_valid(page_req))\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_INVALID_ARGUMENT, \"Invalid pagination request\");\n        return SIZE_MAX;\n    }\n\n    if (pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&state->lock) != 0)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_INTERNAL, \"Failed to acquire read lock\");\n        return SIZE_MAX;\n    }\n\n    size_t fetched = repository_list_textures(state->repo, page_req, out_list, err);\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&state->lock);\n\n    if (fetched == SIZE_MAX)\n        return SIZE_MAX;\n\n    if (state->metrics)\n        metrics_histogram_observe(state->metrics, \"textures_per_page\", fetched);\n\n    log_debug(\"[texture-handler] Listed %zu textures (page %u)\", fetched, page_req->page_index);\n    return fetched;\n}\n\n/**\n * Deletes a texture by ID.  Returns true on success, false otherwise.\n */\nbool texture_handler_delete(TextureHandler *handler, const char *texture_id, TextureError *err)\n{\n    TextureHandlerState *state = (TextureHandlerState *)handler;\n    if (!state || !state->ready)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_NOT_INITIALIZED, \"TextureHandler is not initialized\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!validation_is_uuid(texture_id))\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_INVALID_ARGUMENT, \"Invalid UUID '%s'\", texture_id);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&state->lock) != 0)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_INTERNAL, \"Failed to acquire write lock\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    bool ok = repository_delete_texture(state->repo, texture_id, err);\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&state->lock);\n\n    if (ok && state->metrics)\n        metrics_counter_inc(state->metrics, \"texture_deleted_total\", 1);\n\n    log_info(\"[texture-handler] Deleted texture '%s' (%s)\", texture_id, ok ? \"ok\" : \"not-found\");\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Private Helpers                                                          */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic void _set_error(TextureError *out, TextureErrorCode code, const char *fmt, ...)\n{\n    if (!out)\n        return;\n\n    va_list ap;\n    va_start(ap, fmt);\n    vsnprintf(out->message, sizeof(out->message), fmt, ap);\n    va_end(ap);\n\n    out->code = code;\n}\n\nstatic bool _validate_texture_input(const TextureInput *in, TextureError *err)\n{\n    if (!in)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_INVALID_ARGUMENT, \"TextureInput cannot be NULL\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!validation_is_valid_name(in->name, MAX_TEXTURE_NAME_LEN))\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_VALIDATION, \"Invalid texture name\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!validation_is_color_profile(in->color_profile))\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_VALIDATION, \"Unsupported color profile '%s'\", in->color_profile);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (in->width == 0 || in->height == 0 || in->width > 16384 || in->height > 16384)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_VALIDATION, \"Texture dimensions out of bounds (1..16384)\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic char *_generate_uuid(void)\n{\n    uuid_t bin;\n    uuid_generate_random(bin);\n\n    char *str = malloc(TEXTURE_ID_STRLEN);\n    if (!str)\n        return NULL;\n\n    uuid_unparse_lower(bin, str);\n    return str;\n}\n\nstatic json_t *_texture_to_json(const Texture *tx, TextureError *err)\n{\n    json_t *obj = json_object();\n    if (!obj)\n    {\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_OOM, \"Failed to allocate JSON object\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    if (json_object_set_new(obj, \"id\",            json_string(tx->id))           < 0 ||\n        json_object_set_new(obj, \"name\",          json_string(tx->name))         < 0 ||\n        json_object_set_new(obj, \"color_profile\", json_string(tx->color_profile))< 0 ||\n        json_object_set_new(obj, \"width\",         json_integer(tx->width))       < 0 ||\n        json_object_set_new(obj, \"height\",        json_integer(tx->height))      < 0 ||\n        json_object_set_new(obj, \"created_at\",    json_integer(tx->created_at))  < 0)\n    {\n        json_decref(obj);\n        _set_error(err, TX_ERR_INTERNAL, \"Failed to build texture JSON payload\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    return obj;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* End of file                                                               */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n","numLines":400,"startLine":1,"totalLines":400}}}

createPalette(input: CreatePaletteInput!): Palette!\n *\n * For simplicity, we forward the entire JSON payload to the palette service.\n */\nstatic json_t *resolve_create_palette(const json_t *args, void *user_data, GQLError **gql_err)\n{\n    gql_context_t *ctx = (gql_context_t *)user_data;\n\n    /* Rate limiting */\n    if (rate_limiter_acquire(ctx->limiter) != 0) {\n        *gql_err = gql_error_new(GQL_ERR_RATE_LIMIT, \"Too many requests\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Validate mandatory fields */\n    const json_t *input = json_object_get(args, \"input\");\n    if (!input || !json_is_object(input)) {\n        *gql_err = gql_error_new(GQL_ERR_VALIDATION, \"`input` must be provided\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    const char *name = json_string_value(json_object_get(input, \"name\"));\n    if (!name || strlen(name) == 0) {\n        *gql_err = gql_error_new(GQL_ERR_VALIDATION, \"`name` cannot be empty\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Serialize input to JSON for POST body */\n    char *body = json_dumps(input, JSON_COMPACT);\n    if (!body) {\n        *gql_err = gql_error_new(GQL_ERR_INTERNAL, \"JSON serialization failed\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Prepare CURL */\n    CURL *curl = curl_easy_init();\n    if (!curl) {\n        free(body);\n        *gql_err = gql_error_new(GQL_ERR_INTERNAL, \"Internal HTTP init error\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    char url[512];\n    snprintf(url, sizeof(url), \"%s/palettes\", PALETTE_SERVICE_BASE_URL);\n\n    struct curl_slist *headers = NULL;\n    headers = curl_slist_append(headers, \"Content-Type: application/json\");\n\n    curl_buffer_t chunk = {.data = NULL, .size = 0};\n\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_URL, url);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_POST, 1L);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, headers);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, body);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDSIZE, (long)strlen(body));\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEFUNCTION, curl_buffer_write);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEDATA, (void *)&chunk);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_TIMEOUT, HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECS);\n\n    LOG_INFO(ctx->log, \"POST %s\", url);\n    CURLcode res = curl_easy_perform(curl);\n    long http_status = 0;\n    curl_easy_getinfo(curl, CURLINFO_RESPONSE_CODE, &http_status);\n\n    curl_easy_cleanup(curl);\n    curl_slist_free_all(headers);\n    free(body);\n\n    if (res != CURLE_OK) {\n        LOG_ERROR(ctx->log, \"curl_easy_perform() failed: %s\", curl_easy_strerror(res));\n        free(chunk.data);\n        *gql_err = gql_error_new(GQL_ERR_NETWORK, \"Upstream network error\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    if (http_status >= 400) {\n        LOG_WARN(ctx->log, \"Upstream error %ld for POST %s\", http_status, url);\n        free(chunk.data);\n        *gql_err = gql_error_new(GQL_ERR_UPSTREAM, \"Upstream service error\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    json_error_t json_err;\n    json_t *json = json_loads(chunk.data, 0, &json_err);\n    free(chunk.data);\n\n    if (!json) {\n        LOG_ERROR(ctx->log, \"JSON parse error at line %d: %s\", json_err.line, json_err.text);\n        *gql_err = gql_error_new(GQL_ERR_INTERNAL, \"Invalid upstream JSON\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    METRIC_INC(ctx->metrics, \"create_palette_resolver_success\");\n    return json;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                           Registration / bootstrap                         */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * gql_register_resolvers:\n *\n * Register all resolvers for the palette sub-graph with the GraphQL runtime.\n */\nint gql_register_resolvers(GraphQLRuntime *runtime,\n                           logger_t *log,\n                           metrics_t *metrics,\n                           rate_limiter_t *limiter)\n{\n    if (!runtime) return -EINVAL;\n\n    gql_context_t *ctx = calloc(1, sizeof(*ctx));\n    if (!ctx) {\n        LOG_ERROR(log, \"Out of memory allocating gql_context_t\");\n        return -ENOMEM;\n    }\n\n    ctx->log     = log;\n    ctx->metrics = metrics;\n    ctx->limiter = limiter;\n\n    int rc = 0;\n\n    rc |= graphql_register_resolver(runtime, \"Query\",  \"palette\",\n                                    resolve_palette,       ctx /*user_data*/);\n\n    rc |= graphql_register_resolver(runtime, \"Query\",  \"palettes\",\n                                    resolve_palettes,      ctx);\n\n    rc |= graphql_register_resolver(runtime, \"Mutation\",\"createPalette\",\n                                    resolve_create_palette, ctx);\n\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        LOG_ERROR(log, \"Failed to register one or more GraphQL resolvers\");\n        free(ctx);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    LOG_INFO(log, \"Palette resolvers registered successfully\");\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                           Graceful shutdown hook                           */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nvoid gql_resolvers_shutdown(GraphQLRuntime *runtime)\n{\n    /* Currently nothing to free. Add schema-wide cleanup here if needed. */\n    (void)runtime;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                   EOF                                      */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n","numLines":408,"startLine":1,"totalLines":408}}}

The analysis is based on examination of the codebase, including the `sc_ipc` library, API Gateway, microservices, and build configuration files.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Component Identification\n\n### 1.1 Current Inter-Service Communication Components\n\nThe SynestheticCanvas system uses a **hybrid communication approach**:\n\n1. **UNIX Domain Socket IPC Library (`sc_ipc`)** - for local inter-process communication\n2. **HTTP/REST over libcurl** - for network-based service-to-service calls\n\n#### Core IPC Library Files\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `libs/sc_ipc/include/sc_ipc.h` | Public API header defining the IPC interface, message types, error codes, and envelope structures |\n| `libs/sc_ipc/src/sc_ipc.c` | Implementation of UNIX domain socket-based IPC with length-prefixed framing |\n\n#### API Gateway Client-Side Communication\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.c` | HTTP/REST client wrapper using libcurl for communicating with downstream microservices |\n| `api-gateway/graphql/resolvers.c` | GraphQL resolvers that invoke `service_client` to call backend services |\n| `api-gateway/src/main.c` | Gateway entry point and HTTP server initialization |\n| `api-gateway/src/gateway_server.c` | HTTP server implementation |\n\n#### Microservice Server-Side Components\n\n| Service | Main Files |\n|---------|-----------|\n| **texture-service** | `services/texture-service/src/main.c` - HTTP daemon using libmicrohttpd |\n| | `services/texture-service/src/texture_handler.c` - Request handling and business logic |\n| | `services/texture-service/src/texture_service.c` - Core texture synthesis logic |\n| | `services/texture-service/src/texture_repository.c` - Data persistence layer |\n| **palette-service** | Similar structure in `services/palette-service/` |\n| **audio-service** | Similar structure in `services/audio-service/` |\n| **narrative-service** | Similar structure in `services/narrative-service/` |\n\n#### Shared Libraries\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `libs/sc_common/include/sc_config.h` | Runtime configuration, feature flags, environment variable parsing |\n| `libs/sc_common/include/sc_logging.h` | Structured logging utilities |\n| `libs/sc_common/include/sc_errors.h` | Error handling framework |\n| `libs/sc_common/include/sc_types.h` | Common type definitions |\n\n### 1.2 Current Communication Architecture\n\nBased on code analysis:\n\n1. **The `sc_ipc` library** provides UNIX domain socket communication with:\n   - Length-prefixed (4-byte big-endian) message framing\n   - Non-blocking I/O with configurable timeouts\n   - Server/client connection patterns\n   - Maximum 64KB payload size\n\n2. **The `service_client.c`** in the API Gateway uses:\n   - libcurl for HTTP/JSON REST calls to microservices\n   - Environment variables for service URLs (e.g., `SC_TEXTURE_SERVICE_URL`)\n   - Exponential backoff retry logic\n   - cJSON for JSON parsing\n\n3. **Microservices** expose HTTP endpoints via libmicrohttpd:\n   - REST endpoints like `/healthz`, `/v1/noise`\n   - JSON request/response format\n\n---\n\n## 2. Data Flow Analysis\n\n### 2.1 End-to-End Request Flow: API Gateway to Texture Service\n\nBased on the `data_flow.puml` and code analysis:\n\n```\n┌──────────┐     HTTPS     ┌─────────────┐     HTTP/JSON    ┌─────────────────┐     SQL     ┌──────────┐\n│  Client  │ ───────────▶ │ API Gateway │ ────────────────▶ │ Texture Service │ ──────────▶ │ Database │\n│          │              │             │                   │                 │             │(Postgres)│\n└──────────┘              └─────────────┘                   └─────────────────┘             └──────────┘\n```\n\n#### Detailed Flow Sequence:\n\n1. **Client Request** (GraphQL or REST)\n   - Client sends HTTPS request to API Gateway\n   - Location: `api-gateway/src/main.c:196` (event loop)\n\n2. **Rate Limiting & Validation**\n   - Token bucket rate limiter checks quota (`rate_limit.h`)\n   - GraphQL schema validation via cgql library\n   - Location: `api-gateway/graphql/resolvers.c:194-196`\n\n3. **Resolver Execution**\n   - GraphQL runtime dispatches to appropriate resolver\n   - Example: `resolve_palette()` at `resolvers.c:184-210`\n\n4. **Service Client Call**\n   - `service_client.c:174-198` (`sc_service_client_call`)\n   - Builds full URL from endpoint base + path\n   - Makes HTTP request via libcurl with retry logic\n\n5. **Key Functions in Request Path:**\n   ```c\n   // API Gateway - resolvers.c\n   static json_t *resolve_palette(const json_t *args, void *user_data, GQLError **gql_err)\n\n   // API Gateway - service_client.c\n   int sc_service_client_call(sc_service_client_t *cli, sc_service_endp_t endpoint, ...)\n   static int perform_request(sc_service_client_t *client, const char *url, ...)\n\n   // Texture Service - main.c\n   static enum MHD_Result request_router(void *cls, struct MHD_Connection *connection, ...)\n\n   // Texture Service - texture_handler.c\n   Texture *texture_handler_get(TextureHandler *handler, const char *texture_id, TextureError *err)\n   ```\n\n6. **Response Path**\n   - Service returns JSON response\n   - Gateway parses and transforms to GraphQL response format\n   - Gateway caches response (Redis, optional TTL)\n   - Client receives final response\n\n#### Key Data Structures:\n\n```c\n// service_client.c\nstruct sc_service_client {\n    char *palette_url;\n    char *texture_url;\n    char *audio_url;\n    char *narrative_url;\n    long timeout_ms;\n    int max_retries;\n    atomic_ullong requests_total;\n    CURL *curl_handle;\n};\n\n// sc_ipc.h (for future reference)\ntypedef struct sc_ipc_msg_hdr {\n    uint32_t magic;           // 0xC0FEBABE\n    uint16_t version;\n    uint16_t type;            // REQUEST, RESPONSE, EVENT, HEARTBEAT\n    uint32_t length;\n    uint64_t correlation_id;\n    uint64_t timestamp_ns;\n} sc_ipc_msg_hdr_t;\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Migration Strategy Proposal\n\n### 3.1 Phase 1: Foundation (Weeks 1-2)\n\n#### 3.1.1 Define gRPC Service Contracts\n\nCreate Protocol Buffer definitions for the texture service:\n\n```protobuf\n// proto/texture.proto\nsyntax = \"proto3\";\n\npackage synestheticcanvas.texture.v1;\n\nservice TextureService {\n    rpc GetTexture(GetTextureRequest) returns (GetTextureResponse);\n    rpc CreateTexture(CreateTextureRequest) returns (CreateTextureResponse);\n    rpc ListTextures(ListTexturesRequest) returns (ListTexturesResponse);\n    rpc DeleteTexture(DeleteTextureRequest) returns (DeleteTextureResponse);\n    rpc GenerateNoise(GenerateNoiseRequest) returns (GenerateNoiseResponse);\n}\n\nmessage GetTextureRequest {\n    string texture_id = 1;\n}\n\nmessage GetTextureResponse {\n    Texture texture = 1;\n}\n\nmessage Texture {\n    string id = 1;\n    string name = 2;\n    string color_profile = 3;\n    uint32 width = 4;\n    uint32 height = 5;\n    uint64 created_at = 6;\n}\n\n// ... additional message definitions\n```\n\n#### 3.1.2 CMakeLists.txt Modifications\n\n**For the texture-service (`services/texture-service/CMakeLists.txt`):**\n\n```cmake\n# Add gRPC/Protobuf dependencies\nfind_package(Protobuf REQUIRED)\nfind_package(gRPC CONFIG REQUIRED)\n\n# Generate C sources from .proto files\nset(PROTO_SRC_DIR ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/proto)\nset(PROTO_FILES ${PROTO_SRC_DIR}/texture.proto)\n\n# Use protoc and grpc_cpp_plugin\nadd_custom_command(\n    OUTPUT texture.pb.c texture.pb-c.h texture.grpc.pb.c texture.grpc.pb-c.h\n    COMMAND ${Protobuf_PROTOC_EXECUTABLE}\n    ARGS --c_out=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}\n         --grpc_out=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}\n         --plugin=protoc-gen-grpc=${gRPC_CPP_PLUGIN_EXECUTABLE}\n         -I${PROTO_SRC_DIR}\n         ${PROTO_FILES}\n    DEPENDS ${PROTO_FILES}\n)\n\n# Add generated files to sources\nset(GRPC_GENERATED_SRC\n    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/texture.pb.c\n    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/texture.grpc.pb.c\n)\n\ntarget_sources(texture_service PRIVATE ${GRPC_GENERATED_SRC})\ntarget_link_libraries(texture_service\n    PUBLIC\n        gRPC::grpc\n        protobuf::libprotobuf-c\n)\n```\n\n**For the API Gateway (`api-gateway/CMakeLists.txt`):**\n\n```cmake\n# Add gRPC client dependencies\nfind_package(gRPC CONFIG REQUIRED)\nfind_package(Protobuf REQUIRED)\n\n# Include generated protobuf headers\ntarget_include_directories(api_gateway PRIVATE ${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/generated)\n\n# Link gRPC client library\ntarget_link_libraries(api_gateway\n    PRIVATE\n        gRPC::grpc\n        protobuf::libprotobuf-c\n)\n```\n\n### 3.2 Phase 2: Parallel Implementation (Weeks 3-4)\n\n#### 3.2.1 Dual-Protocol Server in Texture Service\n\nModify `services/texture-service/src/main.c` to support both HTTP and gRPC simultaneously:\n\n```c\n// main.c - Conceptual structure for dual-protocol support\n#include <grpc/grpc.h>\n#include <microhttpd.h>\n\ntypedef struct {\n    struct MHD_Daemon *http_daemon;  // Existing HTTP server\n    grpc_server *grpc_server;        // New gRPC server\n    ServiceConfig cfg;\n} DualServiceCtx;\n\n// gRPC handler (new)\nstatic grpc_call_error handle_grpc_get_texture(\n    grpc_call *call,\n    GetTextureRequest *req,\n    GetTextureResponse **resp\n) {\n    // Reuse existing handler logic\n    TextureError err = {0};\n    Texture *tx = texture_handler_get(g_handler, req->texture_id, &err);\n    if (!tx) {\n        return grpc_call_error_from_texture_error(&err);\n    }\n    *resp = texture_to_grpc_response(tx);\n    texture_free(tx);\n    return GRPC_CALL_OK;\n}\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    DualServiceCtx svc;\n    config_set_defaults(&svc.cfg);\n\n    // Start HTTP daemon on default port (existing)\n    svc.http_daemon = MHD_start_daemon(\n        MHD_USE_AUTO | MHD_USE_INTERNAL_POLLING_THREAD,\n        svc.cfg.port,\n        NULL, NULL,\n        request_router, &svc,\n        MHD_OPTION_END\n    );\n\n    // Start gRPC server on separate port (new)\n    uint16_t grpc_port = svc.cfg.port + 1000;  // e.g., 9080 if HTTP is 8080\n    svc.grpc_server = grpc_server_create(NULL, NULL);\n    grpc_server_add_http2_port(svc.grpc_server,\n        format_address(\"0.0.0.0\", grpc_port),\n        grpc_insecure_server_credentials_create());\n\n    grpc_server_register_method(svc.grpc_server,\n        \"/texture.v1.TextureService/GetTexture\",\n        handle_grpc_get_texture);\n\n    grpc_server_start(svc.grpc_server);\n\n    log_msg(LOG_INFO, \"Dual-protocol server: HTTP=%u, gRPC=%u\",\n            svc.cfg.port, grpc_port);\n\n    // Event loop handles both...\n    while (!g_shutdown) sleep(1);\n\n    // Graceful shutdown\n    MHD_stop_daemon(svc.http_daemon);\n    grpc_server_shutdown_and_notify(svc.grpc_server, NULL, NULL);\n    grpc_server_destroy(svc.grpc_server);\n\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```\n\n#### 3.2.2 Feature Flag in API Gateway Configuration\n\nExtend `sc_config.h` and `service_client.c`:\n\n```c\n// In sc_config.h - add feature toggle\n#ifndef SC_FEATURE_GRPC_TEXTURE\n#   define SC_FEATURE_GRPC_TEXTURE 0  // Off by default during migration\n#endif\n\n// Environment variable override\nstatic inline bool sc_use_grpc_for_service(const char *service_name) {\n    char env_var[64];\n    snprintf(env_var, sizeof(env_var), \"SC_USE_GRPC_%s\", service_name);\n    return sc_bool_from_env(env_var, false);\n}\n```\n\n```c\n// In service_client.c - protocol selection logic\nint sc_service_client_call(sc_service_client_t *cli,\n                           sc_service_endp_t endpoint,\n                           const char *method,\n                           const char *path,\n                           const char *request_json,\n                           cJSON **response_json,\n                           long *http_status)\n{\n    // Check if gRPC is enabled for this service\n    if (endpoint == SC_EP_TEXTURE && sc_use_grpc_for_service(\"TEXTURE\")) {\n        return sc_service_client_call_grpc(cli, endpoint, method, path,\n                                           request_json, response_json, http_status);\n    }\n\n    // Fallback to existing HTTP/REST implementation\n    return sc_service_client_call_http(cli, endpoint, method, path,\n                                       request_json, response_json, http_status);\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 Phase 3: Gradual Rollout (Weeks 5-6)\n\n#### 3.3.1 Deployment Strategy\n\n1. **Canary Deployment**\n   - Deploy texture-service with dual-protocol support\n   - Set `SC_USE_GRPC_TEXTURE=false` initially\n   - Route 5% of traffic to gRPC via feature flag\n\n2. **Incremental Traffic Shift**\n   - Week 5: 5% → 25% → 50%\n   - Week 6: 75% → 100%\n   - Monitor latency, error rates, resource usage\n\n3. **Rollback Capability**\n   - `SC_USE_GRPC_TEXTURE=false` instantly reverts to HTTP\n   - No service restart required\n\n### 3.4 Phase 4: Consolidation (Weeks 7-8)\n\n1. Remove HTTP endpoints from texture-service\n2. Update API Gateway to use gRPC exclusively for texture-service\n3. Remove dual-protocol code, simplify to gRPC-only\n4. Repeat phases 2-4 for remaining services (palette, audio, narrative)\n\n### 3.5 New gRPC-Based Communication Flow\n\n```\n┌──────────┐   GraphQL/REST   ┌─────────────┐      gRPC/HTTP/2      ┌─────────────────┐\n│  Client  │ ───────────────▶ │ API Gateway │ ─────────────────────▶ │ Texture Service │\n│          │                  │             │                        │                 │\n└──────────┘                  └─────────────┘                        └─────────────────┘\n                                    │\n                                    │ Protocol Buffer\n                                    │ serialization\n                                    ▼\n                              ┌─────────────┐\n                              │ gRPC Client │\n                              │   Stub      │\n                              └─────────────┘\n```\n\n**Key Changes:**\n- Binary Protocol Buffers replace JSON serialization\n- HTTP/2 enables multiplexed streaming\n- Strongly-typed service contracts via `.proto` files\n- Built-in load balancing and service discovery options\n\n---\n\n## 4. Risk Assessment\n\n### 4.1 Build System Complexity (HIGH RISK)\n\n**Risk:** Integrating gRPC into a C project via CMake is non-trivial.\n\n**Details:**\n- gRPC's primary support is for C++, not C. The C bindings (gRPC Core) have limited documentation.\n- Protobuf code generation requires `protobuf-c` (a third-party C protobuf library), not the official Google `protobuf`.\n- FetchContent patterns for gRPC often fail due to complex transitive dependencies (abseil, re2, etc.).\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Use system-installed gRPC packages (`apt install libgrpc-dev`) rather than FetchContent\n- Consider gRPC-C alternatives like `grpc_c` or `protobuf-c-rpc`\n- Extensive pre-merge CI testing of the build system changes\n\n**Evidence from codebase:**\n- Current CMakeLists.txt files use FetchContent for simple dependencies (cJSON, libgraphqlparser)\n- No existing gRPC usage means no precedent for integration patterns\n\n### 4.2 Performance Characteristics (MEDIUM RISK)\n\n**Risk:** gRPC may introduce latency overhead compared to the custom IPC.\n\n**Details:**\n- The current `sc_ipc` library uses UNIX domain sockets with a lean 4-byte length-prefix framing\n- gRPC adds HTTP/2 framing, Protobuf encoding/decoding, and connection management overhead\n- For intra-host communication, UNIX sockets typically outperform TCP-based gRPC\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Benchmark before/after migration (target: <10% latency increase)\n- Consider gRPC over UNIX domain sockets for intra-host calls\n- Use connection pooling and keep-alive to reduce handshake overhead\n\n**Evidence from codebase:**\n- `sc_ipc.h:62-64` specifies max 64KB payloads and UNIX domain socket transport\n- Performance target from `overview.md:17`: \"Frame-accurate (< 16 ms) responses\"\n\n### 4.3 Operational Complexity During Transition (MEDIUM RISK)\n\n**Risk:** Running dual-protocol systems increases operational burden.\n\n**Details:**\n- Configuration management doubles (HTTP endpoints + gRPC ports)\n- Monitoring/alerting must cover both protocols\n- Load balancers need updating (gRPC requires HTTP/2-aware proxying)\n- Service discovery must track additional endpoints\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Use environment variables with sensible defaults\n- Implement health checks for both protocols\n- Phase transition quickly (6-8 weeks target)\n- Document operational runbooks for both modes\n\n**Evidence from codebase:**\n- Service configuration via environment variables (`sc_config.h:204-215`)\n- Health endpoint pattern: `/healthz` returns `{\"status\":\"ok\"}` (`main.c:289-294`)\n\n### 4.4 Code Divergence Risk (MEDIUM RISK)\n\n**Risk:** Maintaining two code paths during migration introduces bugs.\n\n**Details:**\n- Handler logic may drift between HTTP and gRPC implementations\n- Testing burden doubles\n- Edge case handling may differ\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Extract business logic into protocol-agnostic functions\n- Comprehensive integration tests covering both paths\n- Short migration window to minimize divergence period\n\n**Evidence from codebase:**\n- Handler logic in `texture_handler.c` is already separated from transport\n- Repository pattern (`texture_repository.c`) enables clean abstraction\n\n### 4.5 Language Ecosystem Friction (LOW-MEDIUM RISK)\n\n**Risk:** gRPC's C ecosystem is less mature than C++ or Go.\n\n**Details:**\n- gRPC Core's C API is marked as \"low-level\" and \"for advanced users\"\n- Documentation focuses on C++, Python, and Go\n- Fewer community examples and Stack Overflow answers\n- May need to drop to gRPC Core primitives for advanced features\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Consider a C++ wrapper layer if C API proves too cumbersome\n- Evaluate alternatives like Cap'n Proto or FlatBuffers with RPC\n- Budget extra time for learning curve\n\n**Evidence from documentation:**\n- `microservices.md` references gRPC but with C++ style (`gen/palette.grpc.pb-c.h`)\n- Current code uses C11/C17 standards without C++ dependencies\n\n---\n\n## 5. Recommendations\n\n### 5.1 Immediate Actions\n\n1. **Proof of Concept:** Build a minimal gRPC server in C for the texture service to validate CMake integration\n2. **Benchmark Baseline:** Measure current HTTP latency, throughput, and resource usage\n3. **Define Proto Contracts:** Create `.proto` files for all four services before implementation begins\n\n### 5.2 Technical Decisions\n\n1. **Use `grpc-c` or gRPC Core + protobuf-c** for C compatibility\n2. **Keep HTTP as fallback** even post-migration for debugging and legacy clients\n3. **Implement gRPC reflection** to enable tools like `grpcurl` for debugging\n\n### 5.3 Success Criteria\n\n| Metric | Target |\n|--------|--------|\n| P95 Latency | < 20ms (max 25% increase from baseline) |\n| Error Rate | < 0.1% during rollout |\n| Migration Duration | < 8 weeks per service |\n| Build Time Increase | < 20% |\n\n---\n\n## 6. The analysis is based on examination of the codebase, including the `sc_ipc` library, API Gateway, microservices, and build configuration files.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Component Identification\n\n### 1.1 Current Inter-Service Communication Components\n\nThe SynestheticCanvas system uses a **hybrid communication approach**:\n\n1. **UNIX Domain Socket IPC Library (`sc_ipc`)** - for local inter-process communication\n2. **HTTP/REST over libcurl** - for network-based service-to-service calls\n\n#### Core IPC Library Files\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `libs/sc_ipc/include/sc_ipc.h` | Public API header defining the IPC interface, message types, error codes, and envelope structures |\n| `libs/sc_ipc/src/sc_ipc.c` | Implementation of UNIX domain socket-based IPC with length-prefixed framing |\n\n#### API Gateway Client-Side Communication\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.c` | HTTP/REST client wrapper using libcurl for communicating with downstream microservices |\n| `api-gateway/graphql/resolvers.c` | GraphQL resolvers that invoke `service_client` to call backend services |\n| `api-gateway/src/main.c` | Gateway entry point and HTTP server initialization |\n| `api-gateway/src/gateway_server.c` | HTTP server implementation |\n\n#### Microservice Server-Side Components\n\n| Service | Main Files |\n|---------|-----------|\n| **texture-service** | `services/texture-service/src/main.c` - HTTP daemon using libmicrohttpd |\n| | `services/texture-service/src/texture_handler.c` - Request handling and business logic |\n| | `services/texture-service/src/texture_service.c` - Core texture synthesis logic |\n| | `services/texture-service/src/texture_repository.c` - Data persistence layer |\n| **palette-service** | Similar structure in `services/palette-service/` |\n| **audio-service** | Similar structure in `services/audio-service/` |\n| **narrative-service** | Similar structure in `services/narrative-service/` |\n\n#### Shared Libraries\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `libs/sc_common/include/sc_config.h` | Runtime configuration, feature flags, environment variable parsing |\n| `libs/sc_common/include/sc_logging.h` | Structured logging utilities |\n| `libs/sc_common/include/sc_errors.h` | Error handling framework |\n| `libs/sc_common/include/sc_types.h` | Common type definitions |\n\n### 1.2 Current Communication Architecture\n\nBased on code analysis:\n\n1. **The `sc_ipc` library** provides UNIX domain socket communication with:\n   - Length-prefixed (4-byte big-endian) message framing\n   - Non-blocking I/O with configurable timeouts\n   - Server/client connection patterns\n   - Maximum 64KB payload size\n\n2. **The `service_client.c`** in the API Gateway uses:\n   - libcurl for HTTP/JSON REST calls to microservices\n   - Environment variables for service URLs (e.g., `SC_TEXTURE_SERVICE_URL`)\n   - Exponential backoff retry logic\n   - cJSON for JSON parsing\n\n3. **Microservices** expose HTTP endpoints via libmicrohttpd:\n   - REST endpoints like `/healthz`, `/v1/noise`\n   - JSON request/response format\n\n---\n\n## 2. Data Flow Analysis\n\n### 2.1 End-to-End Request Flow: API Gateway to Texture Service\n\nBased on the `data_flow.puml` and code analysis:\n\n```\n┌──────────┐     HTTPS     ┌─────────────┐     HTTP/JSON    ┌─────────────────┐     SQL     ┌──────────┐\n│  Client  │ ───────────▶ │ API Gateway │ ────────────────▶ │ Texture Service │ ──────────▶ │ Database │\n│          │              │             │                   │                 │             │(Postgres)│\n└──────────┘              └─────────────┘                   └─────────────────┘             └──────────┘\n```\n\n#### Detailed Flow Sequence:\n\n1. **Client Request** (GraphQL or REST)\n   - Client sends HTTPS request to API Gateway\n   - Location: `api-gateway/src/main.c:196` (event loop)\n\n2. **Rate Limiting & Validation**\n   - Token bucket rate limiter checks quota (`rate_limit.h`)\n   - GraphQL schema validation via cgql library\n   - Location: `api-gateway/graphql/resolvers.c:194-196`\n\n3. **Resolver Execution**\n   - GraphQL runtime dispatches to appropriate resolver\n   - Example: `resolve_palette()` at `resolvers.c:184-210`\n\n4. **Service Client Call**\n   - `service_client.c:174-198` (`sc_service_client_call`)\n   - Builds full URL from endpoint base + path\n   - Makes HTTP request via libcurl with retry logic\n\n5. **Key Functions in Request Path:**\n   ```c\n   // API Gateway - resolvers.c\n   static json_t *resolve_palette(const json_t *args, void *user_data, GQLError **gql_err)\n\n   // API Gateway - service_client.c\n   int sc_service_client_call(sc_service_client_t *cli, sc_service_endp_t endpoint, ...)\n   static int perform_request(sc_service_client_t *client, const char *url, ...)\n\n   // Texture Service - main.c\n   static enum MHD_Result request_router(void *cls, struct MHD_Connection *connection, ...)\n\n   // Texture Service - texture_handler.c\n   Texture *texture_handler_get(TextureHandler *handler, const char *texture_id, TextureError *err)\n   ```\n\n6. **Response Path**\n   - Service returns JSON response\n   - Gateway parses and transforms to GraphQL response format\n   - Gateway caches response (Redis, optional TTL)\n   - Client receives final response\n\n#### Key Data Structures:\n\n```c\n// service_client.c\nstruct sc_service_client {\n    char *palette_url;\n    char *texture_url;\n    char *audio_url;\n    char *narrative_url;\n    long timeout_ms;\n    int max_retries;\n    atomic_ullong requests_total;\n    CURL *curl_handle;\n};\n\n// sc_ipc.h (for future reference)\ntypedef struct sc_ipc_msg_hdr {\n    uint32_t magic;           // 0xC0FEBABE\n    uint16_t version;\n    uint16_t type;            // REQUEST, RESPONSE, EVENT, HEARTBEAT\n    uint32_t length;\n    uint64_t correlation_id;\n    uint64_t timestamp_ns;\n} sc_ipc_msg_hdr_t;\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Migration Strategy Proposal\n\n### 3.1 Phase 1: Foundation (Weeks 1-2)\n\n#### 3.1.1 Define gRPC Service Contracts\n\nCreate Protocol Buffer definitions for the texture service:\n\n```protobuf\n// proto/texture.proto\nsyntax = \"proto3\";\n\npackage synestheticcanvas.texture.v1;\n\nservice TextureService {\n    rpc GetTexture(GetTextureRequest) returns (GetTextureResponse);\n    rpc CreateTexture(CreateTextureRequest) returns (CreateTextureResponse);\n    rpc ListTextures(ListTexturesRequest) returns (ListTexturesResponse);\n    rpc DeleteTexture(DeleteTextureRequest) returns (DeleteTextureResponse);\n    rpc GenerateNoise(GenerateNoiseRequest) returns (GenerateNoiseResponse);\n}\n\nmessage GetTextureRequest {\n    string texture_id = 1;\n}\n\nmessage GetTextureResponse {\n    Texture texture = 1;\n}\n\nmessage Texture {\n    string id = 1;\n    string name = 2;\n    string color_profile = 3;\n    uint32 width = 4;\n    uint32 height = 5;\n    uint64 created_at = 6;\n}\n\n// ... additional message definitions\n```\n\n#### 3.1.2 CMakeLists.txt Modifications\n\n**For the texture-service (`services/texture-service/CMakeLists.txt`):**\n\n```cmake\n# Add gRPC/Protobuf dependencies\nfind_package(Protobuf REQUIRED)\nfind_package(gRPC CONFIG REQUIRED)\n\n# Generate C sources from .proto files\nset(PROTO_SRC_DIR ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/proto)\nset(PROTO_FILES ${PROTO_SRC_DIR}/texture.proto)\n\n# Use protoc and grpc_cpp_plugin\nadd_custom_command(\n    OUTPUT texture.pb.c texture.pb-c.h texture.grpc.pb.c texture.grpc.pb-c.h\n    COMMAND ${Protobuf_PROTOC_EXECUTABLE}\n    ARGS --c_out=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}\n         --grpc_out=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}\n         --plugin=protoc-gen-grpc=${gRPC_CPP_PLUGIN_EXECUTABLE}\n         -I${PROTO_SRC_DIR}\n         ${PROTO_FILES}\n    DEPENDS ${PROTO_FILES}\n)\n\n# Add generated files to sources\nset(GRPC_GENERATED_SRC\n    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/texture.pb.c\n    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/texture.grpc.pb.c\n)\n\ntarget_sources(texture_service PRIVATE ${GRPC_GENERATED_SRC})\ntarget_link_libraries(texture_service\n    PUBLIC\n        gRPC::grpc\n        protobuf::libprotobuf-c\n)\n```\n\n**For the API Gateway (`api-gateway/CMakeLists.txt`):**\n\n```cmake\n# Add gRPC client dependencies\nfind_package(gRPC CONFIG REQUIRED)\nfind_package(Protobuf REQUIRED)\n\n# Include generated protobuf headers\ntarget_include_directories(api_gateway PRIVATE ${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/generated)\n\n# Link gRPC client library\ntarget_link_libraries(api_gateway\n    PRIVATE\n        gRPC::grpc\n        protobuf::libprotobuf-c\n)\n```\n\n### 3.2 Phase 2: Parallel Implementation (Weeks 3-4)\n\n#### 3.2.1 Dual-Protocol Server in Texture Service\n\nModify `services/texture-service/src/main.c` to support both HTTP and gRPC simultaneously:\n\n```c\n// main.c - Conceptual structure for dual-protocol support\n#include <grpc/grpc.h>\n#include <microhttpd.h>\n\ntypedef struct {\n    struct MHD_Daemon *http_daemon;  // Existing HTTP server\n    grpc_server *grpc_server;        // New gRPC server\n    ServiceConfig cfg;\n} DualServiceCtx;\n\n// gRPC handler (new)\nstatic grpc_call_error handle_grpc_get_texture(\n    grpc_call *call,\n    GetTextureRequest *req,\n    GetTextureResponse **resp\n) {\n    // Reuse existing handler logic\n    TextureError err = {0};\n    Texture *tx = texture_handler_get(g_handler, req->texture_id, &err);\n    if (!tx) {\n        return grpc_call_error_from_texture_error(&err);\n    }\n    *resp = texture_to_grpc_response(tx);\n    texture_free(tx);\n    return GRPC_CALL_OK;\n}\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    DualServiceCtx svc;\n    config_set_defaults(&svc.cfg);\n\n    // Start HTTP daemon on default port (existing)\n    svc.http_daemon = MHD_start_daemon(\n        MHD_USE_AUTO | MHD_USE_INTERNAL_POLLING_THREAD,\n        svc.cfg.port,\n        NULL, NULL,\n        request_router, &svc,\n        MHD_OPTION_END\n    );\n\n    // Start gRPC server on separate port (new)\n    uint16_t grpc_port = svc.cfg.port + 1000;  // e.g., 9080 if HTTP is 8080\n    svc.grpc_server = grpc_server_create(NULL, NULL);\n    grpc_server_add_http2_port(svc.grpc_server,\n        format_address(\"0.0.0.0\", grpc_port),\n        grpc_insecure_server_credentials_create());\n\n    grpc_server_register_method(svc.grpc_server,\n        \"/texture.v1.TextureService/GetTexture\",\n        handle_grpc_get_texture);\n\n    grpc_server_start(svc.grpc_server);\n\n    log_msg(LOG_INFO, \"Dual-protocol server: HTTP=%u, gRPC=%u\",\n            svc.cfg.port, grpc_port);\n\n    // Event loop handles both...\n    while (!g_shutdown) sleep(1);\n\n    // Graceful shutdown\n    MHD_stop_daemon(svc.http_daemon);\n    grpc_server_shutdown_and_notify(svc.grpc_server, NULL, NULL);\n    grpc_server_destroy(svc.grpc_server);\n\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```\n\n#### 3.2.2 Feature Flag in API Gateway Configuration\n\nExtend `sc_config.h` and `service_client.c`:\n\n```c\n// In sc_config.h - add feature toggle\n#ifndef SC_FEATURE_GRPC_TEXTURE\n#   define SC_FEATURE_GRPC_TEXTURE 0  // Off by default during migration\n#endif\n\n// Environment variable override\nstatic inline bool sc_use_grpc_for_service(const char *service_name) {\n    char env_var[64];\n    snprintf(env_var, sizeof(env_var), \"SC_USE_GRPC_%s\", service_name);\n    return sc_bool_from_env(env_var, false);\n}\n```\n\n```c\n// In service_client.c - protocol selection logic\nint sc_service_client_call(sc_service_client_t *cli,\n                           sc_service_endp_t endpoint,\n                           const char *method,\n                           const char *path,\n                           const char *request_json,\n                           cJSON **response_json,\n                           long *http_status)\n{\n    // Check if gRPC is enabled for this service\n    if (endpoint == SC_EP_TEXTURE && sc_use_grpc_for_service(\"TEXTURE\")) {\n        return sc_service_client_call_grpc(cli, endpoint, method, path,\n                                           request_json, response_json, http_status);\n    }\n\n    // Fallback to existing HTTP/REST implementation\n    return sc_service_client_call_http(cli, endpoint, method, path,\n                                       request_json, response_json, http_status);\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 Phase 3: Gradual Rollout (Weeks 5-6)\n\n#### 3.3.1 Deployment Strategy\n\n1. **Canary Deployment**\n   - Deploy texture-service with dual-protocol support\n   - Set `SC_USE_GRPC_TEXTURE=false` initially\n   - Route 5% of traffic to gRPC via feature flag\n\n2. **Incremental Traffic Shift**\n   - Week 5: 5% → 25% → 50%\n   - Week 6: 75% → 100%\n   - Monitor latency, error rates, resource usage\n\n3. **Rollback Capability**\n   - `SC_USE_GRPC_TEXTURE=false` instantly reverts to HTTP\n   - No service restart required\n\n### 3.4 Phase 4: Consolidation (Weeks 7-8)\n\n1. Remove HTTP endpoints from texture-service\n2. Update API Gateway to use gRPC exclusively for texture-service\n3. Remove dual-protocol code, simplify to gRPC-only\n4. Repeat phases 2-4 for remaining services (palette, audio, narrative)\n\n### 3.5 New gRPC-Based Communication Flow\n\n```\n┌──────────┐   GraphQL/REST   ┌─────────────┐      gRPC/HTTP/2      ┌─────────────────┐\n│  Client  │ ───────────────▶ │ API Gateway │ ─────────────────────▶ │ Texture Service │\n│          │                  │             │                        │                 │\n└──────────┘                  └─────────────┘                        └─────────────────┘\n                                    │\n                                    │ Protocol Buffer\n                                    │ serialization\n                                    ▼\n                              ┌─────────────┐\n                              │ gRPC Client │\n                              │   Stub      │\n                              └─────────────┘\n```\n\n**Key Changes:**\n- Binary Protocol Buffers replace JSON serialization\n- HTTP/2 enables multiplexed streaming\n- Strongly-typed service contracts via `.proto` files\n- Built-in load balancing and service discovery options\n\n---\n\n## 4. Risk Assessment\n\n### 4.1 Build System Complexity (HIGH RISK)\n\n**Risk:** Integrating gRPC into a C project via CMake is non-trivial.\n\n**Details:**\n- gRPC's primary support is for C++, not C. The C bindings (gRPC Core) have limited documentation.\n- Protobuf code generation requires `protobuf-c` (a third-party C protobuf library), not the official Google `protobuf`.\n- FetchContent patterns for gRPC often fail due to complex transitive dependencies (abseil, re2, etc.).\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Use system-installed gRPC packages (`apt install libgrpc-dev`) rather than FetchContent\n- Consider gRPC-C alternatives like `grpc_c` or `protobuf-c-rpc`\n- Extensive pre-merge CI testing of the build system changes\n\n**Evidence from codebase:**\n- Current CMakeLists.txt files use FetchContent for simple dependencies (cJSON, libgraphqlparser)\n- No existing gRPC usage means no precedent for integration patterns\n\n### 4.2 Performance Characteristics (MEDIUM RISK)\n\n**Risk:** gRPC may introduce latency overhead compared to the custom IPC.\n\n**Details:**\n- The current `sc_ipc` library uses UNIX domain sockets with a lean 4-byte length-prefix framing\n- gRPC adds HTTP/2 framing, Protobuf encoding/decoding, and connection management overhead\n- For intra-host communication, UNIX sockets typically outperform TCP-based gRPC\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Benchmark before/after migration (target: <10% latency increase)\n- Consider gRPC over UNIX domain sockets for intra-host calls\n- Use connection pooling and keep-alive to reduce handshake overhead\n\n**Evidence from codebase:**\n- `sc_ipc.h:62-64` specifies max 64KB payloads and UNIX domain socket transport\n- Performance target from `overview.md:17`: \"Frame-accurate (< 16 ms) responses\"\n\n### 4.3 Operational Complexity During Transition (MEDIUM RISK)\n\n**Risk:** Running dual-protocol systems increases operational burden.\n\n**Details:**\n- Configuration management doubles (HTTP endpoints + gRPC ports)\n- Monitoring/alerting must cover both protocols\n- Load balancers need updating (gRPC requires HTTP/2-aware proxying)\n- Service discovery must track additional endpoints\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Use environment variables with sensible defaults\n- Implement health checks for both protocols\n- Phase transition quickly (6-8 weeks target)\n- Document operational runbooks for both modes\n\n**Evidence from codebase:**\n- Service configuration via environment variables (`sc_config.h:204-215`)\n- Health endpoint pattern: `/healthz` returns `{\"status\":\"ok\"}` (`main.c:289-294`)\n\n### 4.4 Code Divergence Risk (MEDIUM RISK)\n\n**Risk:** Maintaining two code paths during migration introduces bugs.\n\n**Details:**\n- Handler logic may drift between HTTP and gRPC implementations\n- Testing burden doubles\n- Edge case handling may differ\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Extract business logic into protocol-agnostic functions\n- Comprehensive integration tests covering both paths\n- Short migration window to minimize divergence period\n\n**Evidence from codebase:**\n- Handler logic in `texture_handler.c` is already separated from transport\n- Repository pattern (`texture_repository.c`) enables clean abstraction\n\n### 4.5 Language Ecosystem Friction (LOW-MEDIUM RISK)\n\n**Risk:** gRPC's C ecosystem is less mature than C++ or Go.\n\n**Details:**\n- gRPC Core's C API is marked as \"low-level\" and \"for advanced users\"\n- Documentation focuses on C++, Python, and Go\n- Fewer community examples and Stack Overflow answers\n- May need to drop to gRPC Core primitives for advanced features\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Consider a C++ wrapper layer if C API proves too cumbersome\n- Evaluate alternatives like Cap'n Proto or FlatBuffers with RPC\n- Budget extra time for learning curve\n\n**Evidence from documentation:**\n- `microservices.md` references gRPC but with C++ style (`gen/palette.grpc.pb-c.h`)\n- Current code uses C11/C17 standards without C++ dependencies\n\n---\n\n## 5. Recommendations\n\n### 5.1 Immediate Actions\n\n1. **Proof of Concept:** Build a minimal gRPC server in C for the texture service to validate CMake integration\n2. **Benchmark Baseline:** Measure current HTTP latency, throughput, and resource usage\n3. **Define Proto Contracts:** Create `.proto` files for all four services before implementation begins\n\n### 5.2 Technical Decisions\n\n1. **Use `grpc-c` or gRPC Core + protobuf-c** for C compatibility\n2. **Keep HTTP as fallback** even post-migration for debugging and legacy clients\n3. **Implement gRPC reflection** to enable tools like `grpcurl` for debugging\n\n### 5.3 Success Criteria\n\n| Metric | Target |\n|--------|--------|\n| P95 Latency | < 20ms (max 25% increase from baseline) |\n| Error Rate | < 0.1% during rollout |\n| Migration Duration | < 8 weeks per service |\n| Build Time Increase | < 20% |\n\n---\n\n## 6. Here's a summary of my findings:\n\n## Summary\n\nThe report at `/logs/agent/solution.md` addresses all four deliverables requested:\n\n### 1. Component Identification\nI identified that the SynestheticCanvas system uses a **hybrid communication approach**:\n- The `sc_ipc` library (`libs/sc_ipc/`) provides UNIX domain socket IPC with length-prefixed framing\n- The API Gateway uses `service_client.c` with libcurl for HTTP/REST calls to microservices\n- Microservices expose HTTP endpoints via libmicrohttpd\n\nKey files identified:\n- `libs/sc_ipc/include/sc_ipc.h` and `src/sc_ipc.c` - IPC library\n- `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.c` - HTTP client\n- `api-gateway/graphql/resolvers.c` - GraphQL resolvers\n- `services/texture-service/src/main.c` - Service entry point\n- `services/texture-service/src/texture_handler.c` - Request handlers\n\n### 2.
